A\n215:   spawn / continue / recovery that keeps failing (or keeps re-triggering on\n216:   freshly-spawned sessions) must STOP: a GLOBAL cap (not just per-session) + a\n217:   dead-mark (a session whose recovery/continue attempt fails is marked dead and\n218:   skipped on later cycles), and freshly auto-spawned sessions must NOT be\n219:   re-scoped as in-scope planners.\n220: - **Acceptance criteria:** (a) under load a failing spawn/continue is globally\n221:   capped and the offending session is dead-marked + skipped (no per-cycle\n222:   retry); (b) auto-spawned sessions are not re-scoped as planners; (c) an\n223:   `UnknownError` on a failed `createUserMessage` discards the session (no\n224:   retry loop); (d) a test/repro showing the loop stops after the cap.\n225: - **Suggested scope:** `.opencode/plugin/auto_resume.ts` — the spawn path\n226:   (unit-4 \"restart→spawn\") + the recovery path; the global cap + dead-mark; the\n227:   scope logic. TIES IN with the earlier design issue (unit-4 scope / the\n228:   spurious worker resume / the action-line dilemma) — all resolved by one scope\n229:   refinement (below).\n230: - **GENERALIZED SCOPE (his ruling 2026-09-22):** unit-4 scope = actual PLANNER\n231:   sessions (always) ∪ sessions started/toggled via `<|Autorun|>` (the #82\n232:   own-line toggle). NO new marker needed — the #82 toggle already gates scope\n233:   ON/OFF; unit 4 just follows it for ANY agent type (drop the planner-only\n234:   gate). (open, 2026-09-23, planner-12 direct; live incident 14:44–14:46Z; HIGH) compaction summary leaks into spawn identity + unit-4 routing (agent=compaction successor; mis-route on a quoted action line)\n302: - **Problem / evidence:** live incident 2026-09-23 14:44–14:46Z (pre-#90 build d2b9d510): planner-11 (ses_f318f0d77…) self-compacted (14:44:19Z) → the compaction summary is stored as an ASSISTANT message with info.agent=compaction + info.model=<session model> (the same-model fallback — agent.compaction.model is commented out). The summary text QUOTES \"Previous planner-10 … closed with `action: restart`\" (6612 chars). The unit-4 tick (14:46:06Z) then: (1) `lastAssistantAction` scanned the summary (the last assistant message) → found the QUOTED `action: restart` → ROUTE= RESTART SPAWN (mis-route — the planner's actual last real turn, 14:44:12Z, was a 76-char no-action-line close; the loop log records \"self-compact + unit-4 resume per maintainer inbox\"); (2) `resolveInjectIdentity` → the summary's info → the spawn body agent=compaction + model=Qwen3.8-27B-Q3S-170K (the spawn= line 14:46:06.647Z). \"compaction\" is NOT an agent defined in the live opencode.jsonc → the successor (ses_f3144d9d6…, ident=…planner-12) ran with the WRONG agent + mode (maintainer: \"i started the session prior with autorun but the wrong agent and mode. thus reverted and then started you with direct\"). DB evidence: the ses_f318f0d77 message 14:44:19.866Z (assistant / agent=compaction) + the auto_resume.log lines 14:46:06.629–647Z.\n303: - **STILL LATENT in the live build (ef8c6149 = #90):** `lastAssistantInfo` (L921-930) + `resolveInjectIdentity` (L1052-1060) + `lastAssistantAction` (L1065-1077) do NOT skip the compaction agent → recurs whenever an autorun session self-compacts immediately before the idle routing decision (the near-limit protocol makes this common): the successor is spawned with the wrong agent, AND the routing reads action lines QUOTED in the summary instead of the real close.\n304: - **Desired outcome:** identity + routing resolve from the last REAL turn (the compaction summary is never used for either).\n305: - **Fix design (recommended — maintainer call, observable behavior change):** skip assistant messages with info.agent === \"compaction\" in BOTH `lastAssistantInfo` (→ the spawn/inject identity falls back to the last real assistant message: the planner's agent+model) and `lastAssistantAction` (→ the routing reads the pre-compact close: a no-action-line Work State dump → recovery, as intended). One guard each; smoke fixture: a messages() shape whose last assistant message = a compaction summary (agent=compaction, text quoting \"action: restart\") → (a) the routing returns null (recovery), (b) the spawn body agent = the preceding real assistant's agent.\n306: - **Acceptance criteria:** the smoke pins above + gate green; LIVE: the next autorun self-compact→idle cycle (a) routes correctly (no mis-spawn from a quoted line), (b) the spawn= line carries agent=planner_* (not compaction).\n307: - **Suggested scope:** `.opencode/plugin/auto_resume.ts` (L921-930, L1052-1060, L1065-1077), `.opencode/plugin/tests/auto_resume.smoke.mjs`.\n308: - **Status:** fix LANDED 2026-09-23 (planner-12 direct, planner-implemented after his \"approved\") — the one-guard-each fix per the design (skip agent=\"compaction\" in `lastAssistantInfo` + `lastAssistantAction`, auto_resume.ts); smoke re-pinned 121/121 (+3 #91 pins e1/e2) + gate green (probe 241/241, all smokes, pytest 459+1w, ruff F=0). Commit **2fa4bb6**. REMAINING — live acceptance (needs his restart to the new build + the next autorun cycle): a self-compact→idle cycle must (a) route from the real close (no mis-spawn from a quoted line), (b) the spawn= line carries agent=planner_* (not compaction) — this also completes the #90 new-build spawn tail (DONE 2026-09-23). **Spawn-tail live acceptance (planner-13, build v=7d2e6207 live, process start 19:13:25Z):** the `spawn=` line carried `agent=planner_Q3S_170K` (the real agent, not compaction) and `route= restart spawn` came from the real close — no mis-route off the quoted 17:32Z compaction summary in this episode. NOT YET live-discriminated: the failure case needs the compaction summary to be the LAST evaluated turn (a self-compact then idle cycle in an autorun-scoped session) — the maintainer's re-engagement + own-line toggle + real close all followed the 17:32Z summary, so the guard was not the deciding factor here; the guard is smoke-covered (3 pins) and the discriminating cycle will occur naturally under the near-limit protocol.\n309: \n310: ## #92. (LANDED 2026-09-27, 2026-09-23, planner-12 direct; his question; SMALL) pre-compaction hook should save BOTH the lossless full markdown AND a raw `--json` snapshot\n311: - **Problem / evidence:** after #78 the hook keeps only the markdown backup. His question (2026-09-23): with raw JSON as the on-demand `--json` mode (DB-sourced), how do you get the pre-compaction JSON later — \"the db is overwritten by the compacted session\"? MEASURED on this session (compacted 17:32:02Z): the DB is NOT overwritten — 36 pre-compaction messages + 170 parts are still in the DB, 76 reasoning parts whole-session (the compaction summary is an ADDED assistant message, agent=compaction; planner-11's session showed the same). So on-demand `--json` of a compacted session works TODAY. BUT the DB is a live host-managed store (session deletion / pruning / migration / corruption can lose the rows) — the hook's _c0 snapshot is the only PINNED \"state at compaction time\" record; markdown-only forfeits the lossless source at exactly that moment.\n312: - **Desired outcome:** every pre-compaction dump writes BOTH: the lossless full markdown (current) + a raw `--json` snapshot (the lossless master — any later filtered view re-derivable from it).\n313: - **Acceptance criteria:** two DUMP-OK lines per dump (md + json); both files land in the archive dir; the #78 diagnostics (ms= / DUMP-RETRY= / DUMP-FAIL + stderr) + the 120 s budget + one retry apply to BOTH dumps; compact_memory smoke re-pinned; gate green.\n314: - **Suggested scope:** `.opencode/plugin/compact_memory.ts` (the preCompactionDump call site — the dump script already has `--json`), `.opencode/plugin/tests/compact_memory.smoke.mjs`.\n315: - **Status:** LANDED 2026-09-27 (worker-26, plan26 — per the 2026-09-27 ruling \"save both\"): `preCompactionDump` now writes BOTH artifacts — the lossless md (first) + the raw `--json` snapshot (the lossless master) — each INDEPENDENTLY (the same attempt → DUMP-RETRY=1 → one retry → DUMP-FAIL per artifact, the #78 120 s budget + stderr capture on both); the DUMP-RETRY=/DUMP-FAIL lines gain the artifact relFile; the hook returns `{ ok, files, error }` (files = the landed paths, md first; error = `<md|json>: <detail>` per failed artifact, joined by \" | \"); the no-overwrite stamp stays MD-base-driven (both artifacts share the stamped base); the dispatch call site reads only ok/error (unchanged). The probe S14 checks 104/105/106/107 re-pinned + NEW check 345 (the json naming byte-exact) — 345/345 (header self-annotated); the compact_memory smoke +2 (78/78: the json artifact exists + the json DUMP-OK line byte-exact); gate green (all smokes, pytest 459 passed + 1 warning, ruff F=0). **Closed (planner-26 bookkeeping, 2026-09-27):** commits `11f4a12` (code) / `5a6e842` (probe) / `e2a1a52` (smoke) / `86bda25` (this status + handover) — planner-verified by own re-run (probe 345/345 + compact_memory smoke 78/78, exit 0). **LIVE-ACCEPTED (planner-27, 2026-09-27, verified from files):** the first real compaction under the #92 build was the planner-27 part-2 self-compact (ctx.log 2026-09-27_15-42/15-45, ses_f1d198b41): TWO DUMP-OK lines (`…_c0.md` ms=49 + `…_c0.json` ms=50) + the COMPACT line (keep=12m tok=29498 computed); both artifacts on disk in `archive/sessions/compaction_dumps/` — the md (616,987 B, correct session/title/agent header) and the json (1,216,263 B, valid JSON, top keys `session,messages,orphan_parts`).\n316: \n317: ## #95. Lesson: don't cite speculative hedges as validated design; evidence should follow the verified path (key dumps).\n247: 2. Tool availability reasoning — I reasoned that get_context_keys \"can't be run this session\" (because it wasn't in my initial tool schema snapshot) and didn't actually try to invoke it. The maintainer said \"just run the probe :-)\" and it worked — tools hot-reload between turns. Lesson: try before concluding unavailability; the tool schema can change mid-session.\n248: 3. Speed of propagation — I quickly edited five files to a half-agreed design (knowledge, TODO, proposal) before the design was locked in; the maintainer had to push back (\"editing everything too fast… let's discuss\"). This is exactly the \"direct session = design exchange\" rule we later codified — it should have been in effect before codification. Lesson: in direct sessions, agree first, then propagate; keep a single-source draft until approval.\n249: 4. Costly read — I read the whole repo's opencode.jsonc (~250 lines including the full agent registry) when I only needed the plugins/compaction keys; ~7K of context burned, and that pushed me into the stop line twice this session. Lesson: for large config files, grep a specific key instead of full reads.\n250: 5. The Object.keys gotcha (technical): prototype methods aren't enumerated — this is recorded in knowledge; as friction: I initially interpreted sessionKeys: [\"_client\"] as \"session doesn't expose methods\" — the typeof check would have caught this earlier. (Actually this is already in knowledge — skip or one line?)\n251: 6. Positive feedback: the get_context_keys / dev_probe_ctx probe pattern was the session's workhorse — small probe tools > armchair reasoning. Worth one positive line.\n252: 7. compact_memory being broken (TODO #52) forced a stop-line handoff twice — the proposal now fixes this; note that the session ended at the stop line twice due to #52.\n253: \n254: ### Task-tool return was a stale mid-session snapshot, not the final message - planner 2026-09-16 (direct session, R1 launch)\n255: The worker session compacted mid-task; the Task tool returned the compaction\n256: point's state dump (open \"Active/Next Move\" items, NO commit hash), while the\n257: session actually continued, finished, and committed green. Now I understand the mechanism:

1. `compact_memory.ts`: `storeMessage` writes the file unconditionally at queue time (before dispatch). The dispatch is fire-and-forget. On verified failure (`compactionFailure(result)` non-empty → console.error, no budget increment), the file remains → zombie.

2. `auto_resume.ts` L1349: `readQueuedMessage(sid)` inside the recovery CONTINUE branch — no COMPACT line check → relays the zombie.

Item 2 options: (a) a relay guard in auto_resume.ts — only relay if a COMPACT line for that sid exists in ctx.log since the file's mtime; or (b) compact_memory deletes the queue file on verified dispatch failure. Option (b) is local and simpler: in the `.then((result) => { const error = compactionFailure(result); if (error) { console.error(...); deleteQueueFile...; return; } })` and `.catch(...)` path, delete the queue file. But wait — "verified dispatch failure" has two faces: (1) the compact call resolves with a failure result (compactionFailure), (2) the compact call rejects (catch). In both cases no compaction occurs → the file should be deleted.

But there's a timing subtlety: `storeMessage` happens before dispatch (in queueMessage at L494/L523, which is called after the `void Promise.resolve(...)` dispatch — actually queueMessage is called synchronously right after dispatch, and the .then callback runs later). So the order is: dispatch (async) → queueMessage stores file → returns response. Later, the .then resolves → failure → delete file. That works: on failure, delete the file.

But also: what about a dispatch that "hangs" (never resolves)? The file remains — the init sweep (item 3) will clean it up by age. OK.

Additionally, item 2 as written in the proposal: "the relay only relays if a COMPACT <sid> line exists in ctx.log since the file's mtime (OR compact_memory deletes the queue file on verified dispatch failure)". The OR gives us a choice. The compact_memory-side deletion is cleaner (no ctx.log parsing needed in auto_resume). I'll implement the compact_memory-side deletion.

Item 3: init-time sweep. Where's auto_resume's init? Let me find it. Also compact_memory.ts — is there an init hook? The plugin's export is `export default async function CompactMemoryPlugin(ctx)` returning `{ tool: {...} }` — no event hook. auto_resume.ts has an init (log trim, lineage restoration). The sweep belongs in auto_resume.ts's init (a place where the DB + temp directory is available). Sweep contents:
- Delete `compact_message_*.consumed` older than N days (N=3? let's pick 3).
- Delete orphaned unconsumed `compact_message_<sid>` files where the sid no longer exists in the DB, or is older than N days.

"sid no longer exists in the DB" — needs a DB read. Does auto_resume's init read the DB? It restores lineage from auto_resume.log, and the watches are built from DB sessions. Let me look at auto_resume's init code.

Actually — a simpler, more robust rule that avoids a DB read: age-based only. "Delete compact_message_*.consumed older than N days, and delete orphaned unconsumed files older than N days". The "sid gone" check is optional (the proposal: "orphaned unconsumed files (sid gone / older than N days)"). Age-only is simpler and has zero DB dependency. A compacted session that's resumed via task_id after 3+ days — unlikely; even if it were, losing the queued message is acceptable (the message is 1–3 lines of context, and the session's committed files are the canonical state). I'll go with age-only, N=3 days.

Hmm wait, but should the sweep go in compact_memory.ts (which owns the file convention) or in auto_resume.ts (which owns the init)? Does compact_memory.ts have an init? Let me check the top of the file and whether there's an `event` hook. The plugin just returns `{tool: ...}`. If I add an event hook for init to compact_memory… the host's plugin API supports `event` hooks (context_recovery uses the `event` hook). But simpler: auto_resume.ts already runs init logic on plugin start (log trim). Let me look at its init section.

Also — which plugin's init is guaranteed to run? Both are registered. auto_resume.ts's init runs on host start. Good.

Let me look at auto_resume.ts's init + readQueuedMessage/consumeQueuedMessage functions.

**Item 3 — Init-time sweep in auto_resume.ts:**

```ts
// compact-message sweep (2026-09-30, items 2+3 of the proposal): age-based cleanup of
// .opencode/temp/compact_message_* files — the compact_memory tool's queued messages.
// Delete `compact_message_*.consumed` older than N days (the tombstone — the evidence for
// the intentional vs. limit-driven discriminator), and unconsumed orphaned files older
// than N days (a failed dispatch left a zombie behind; a compaction that never got resumed
// stays stranded). Age-only: sid-existence check is unnecessary — a session that gets
// resumed after N days loses at most its 1-3 line continuation hint.
// Best-effort: a sweep failure must not break plugin loading.
function sweepQueuedMessages(maxAgeDays: number): void {
  let entries: string[];
  try {
    entries = readdirSync(logDir);
  } catch {
    return;
  }
  const cutoff = Date.now() - maxAgeDays * 24 * 3600 * 1000;
  let swept = 0;
  for (const name of entries) {
    if (!name.startsWith("compact_message_")) continue;
    const full = join(logDir, name);
    try {
      if (statSync(full).mtimeMs <= cutoff) {
        unlinkSync(full);
        swept += 1;
      }
    } catch {
      // best-effort
    }
  }
  if (swept > 0) log(`queue-sweep= swept=${swept} maxAgeDays=${maxAgeDays}`);
}
```
